Implement `fft_conv1(a, L)`:
```
def fft_conv1(a, L=None):
    n=len(a)
    if L is None: L=1 << (2*n-2).bit_length()
    A=fft(a, n? L)
    c=ifft(A*A).real[:2*n-1]
    return c, L
```

Mirror update details:
```
def mirror_step(a, c, L, active, weights, beta, rng=None):
    lam = np.zeros(L)
    lam[active] = weights
    # normalize active sum to 1? If weights already sum1.
    A=fft(a, L); H=fft(lam, L)
    grad = 2 * np.real(ifft(H * np.conj(A)))[:n]
    maxg = grad.max()
    if maxg <=0: return a
    # To keep total, update a * exp(-beta*grad/maxg), then normalize
    fac = np.exp(-beta * grad / maxg)
    a_new = a * fac
    s = a_new.sum()
    if s <=0: return a
    a_new *= 1/s # keep sum1
    return a_new
```
But if a has zeros, factor zero? If a_i=0, a_new_i=0. Good. Factor for high grad can be very small; no underflow if beta small.

Simpler: weights = c[active] ** p / sum (p=2 or 4) to emphasize peaks. If c positive all, top values. Use p=1? For active set from top k, use weights = c[active]/sum. This puts more on highest.    - If no improvement in a full round, perturb: pick top 10 contributors, random multiplier exp(rng.normal(0,0.5)), update via coordinate eval (choose best among some q), or full random.
- Need update c after rescale; ensure no cumulative error. Candidate evaluation must use current c and S.

Coordinate update candidate implementation detail:
```
def coordinate_try(a, c, S, i, q_values):
    old = a[i]
    if old ==0: return None...
    lo=i; hi=min(2*n-2, i+n-1)
    length=hi-lo+1
    partner = lo - i + np.arange(length)  # 0..length-1
    base = c[lo:hi+1]
    coeff = a[partner]
    dq = q_values - 1.0
    # affected = base[None,:] + (2*old*dq)[:,None]*coeff[None,:]
    # diag if 2n?
    vals = base[np.newaxis,:].copy() + (2*old*dq)[:,None]*coeff[np.newaxis,:]
    diag_pos = 2*i - lo
    if 0 <= diag_pos < length:
        dq2 = (old*dq)**2
        vals[:, diag_pos] += dq2
    max_aff = vals.max(axis=1)
    unmax = max(c[:lo].max() if lo>0 else 0.0, c[hi+1:].max() if hi+1 < c.size else 0.0)
    ns = S + old*dq
    # Avoid ns<=0
    valid = ns > 1e-12
    scores = 2*n*np.maximum(max_aff, unmax) / (ns*ns) valid else inf
    best_idx = argmin
    return q_values[best_idx], min(score)
```
Memory vals (m,length). m maybe 7. Use float64. For speed, if q_values fixed, preallocate? Simpler.
After update with best q:
```
x=old*q; d=x-old
c[lo:hi+1] += 2*d*a[partner]
if diag: c[2*i] += d*d
a[i]=x; S += d
```
But note if q=0, d=-old; coeff partner includes a[i]=old at diag; update c diag: base c includes old^2; add 2(-old)*old + old^2 = -old^2, correct. For offdiag, add -2old*a_j, c becomes old terms without i; correct.
After rescale, `a *= 1/S; c *= 1/(S*S); S=1.0`.

Pseudo for mirror_optimize:
```
def run_mirror(a, L, n, start_time, max_time, rng, target_score):
    a=normalize_sum1(a); c=conv; best=score; curr=best; beta=0.1; k=32; stall=0
    while time < max_time:
        cmax=c.max(); score=2*n*cmax
        if score < best: best=score; best_a=a.copy(); stall=0
        else: stall+=1
        # choose k based on stage: min(512, 32+stall? or it//10)
        it +=1; k=min(512, 32+it//20)
        active, weights = choose_active(c,k)
        grad=corr_grad(a, weights, active, L, n)
        maxg=max(grad)
        if maxg small: break
        # if stall>50, adjust beta or perturb
        if stall>100:
            beta = 0.05*rng.uniform(0.5,3)
            k = int(rng.choice([16,64,256,512]))
        # try current beta plus maybe a random beta if stall
        betas = [beta]
        if stall>20 and rng.random()<0.3: betas.append(min(2.0,beta*3))
        best_new_score=inf; best_new_a=None
        for b in betas:
            fac=np.exp(-b*grad/maxg)
            an=a*fac; s=an.sum(); if s<=0 continue; an/=s
            cn=conv(an,L); sc=2n*cn.max()
            if sc<best_new_score: store
        if best_new_score is not None:
            # accept if improves current or best-epsilon or rng
            if best_new_score < curr * (1+1e-6) or rng.random()<0.1:
                 a,best_new_a; c=cn; curr=best_new_score
                 if best_new_score < curr? wait curr variable.
                 # adapt beta if improved: beta*=1.2; else beta*=0.8
            else:
                 beta*=0.8
        if best<=target_score*0.99 and stall large: maybe break? Continue to improve.
```
Need be careful: If we accept non-improvement to current but better than best? best is min; acceptance non-improve cannot beat best. But for exploration, accept if `best_new_score < best + 0.01` maybe. But losing current too much may waste time. Use deterministic Accept if improves current. Exploration via perturb separate.

We can generate in time with sum of sinusoids:
```
def structured_random(n,rng, kind):
    t=np.linspace(0,1,n,endpoint=False)
    a=1.0
    for f_amp in [(1,0.5),(2,0.3),(3,0.2)]:

def scale_for_output(a):
    a=np.maximum(a,0)
    s=a.sum()
    mx=a.max()
    if s<=1e-12 or mx<=0: return fallback
    target_sum = 10.0  # >0.01

    # after scaling, ensure sum>0.01 if possible
    if a.sum()*scale <= 0.01:
        scale = max(scale, 0.01/(a.sum()+1e-300), 1000/mx)

```

`normalize_sum1(a)`:
```
def normalize_sum1(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).copy()
    a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
    s=a.sum()
    if s <= 1e-300:
       return np.ones(len(a),dtype=np.float64)/len(a)
    return a/s
```

`fft_convolve_normalized(a, L=None)`:
```
def fft_convolve_normalized(a, L=None):
    n=len(a)
    if L is None: L=1 << (2*n - 2).bit_length()
    A=fft(a, L)
    c=ifft(A*A).real
    c=c[:2*n-1]
    # remove tiny imaginary? real already. Clip negative small
    # Because A*A for linear convolution, numerical negative small
    if c.size:
        negative = c < 0
        if np.any(negative):
             # clip only if min > -1e-9? Set all negative to 0 might bias but nonnegative true
             c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    return c
```
Clipping all negatives could hide large negative due bug, but true nonnegative. Use `c=np.maximum(c,0)`. The FFT product A*A can yield values that should be nonnegative but due to phase maybe F? For convolution of nonnegative, coefficients nonnegative; roundoff small. Good.

`compute_top_weights(c, k)`:
```
def compute_top_weights(c, k):
    m=len(c)
    if m ==0: return np.array([],int), np.array([])
    if k <=0: return ...
    if k >= m:
        idx=np.arange(m)
    else:
        idx=np.argpartition(c, m-k)[:k]  # m-k where? np.argpartition(a, kth) such that kth element placed; to get k largest use kth=m-k. If k=1, kth=m-1.
    vals=c[idx]
    # weights square
    w=vals*vals
    ws=w.sum()
    if ws <=0:
       w=np.ones_like(vals)
       ws=w.sum()
    w /= ws
    return idx, w
```
If k=0, None.
Argpartition with `m-k` if k<m. If k=1, m-1 okay. If m-k=0? k=m handled. Need if m-k <0 no.

`gradient_from_weights(a, L, active, weights, n)`:
```
def gradient_from_weights(a, L, active, weights, n):
    lam=np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
    # active may include indices beyond 2n-2? no
    lam[active] = weights
    A=fft(a, L)
    H=fft(lam, L)
    grad = np.real(ifft(H * np.conj(A)))[:n]
    return 2.0 * grad
```
Potential issue: Since a and lam real, ifft result real. For circular correlation with zero padding L≥2n-1, indices i+j up to 2n-2 < L, no wrap. Good.

Could optimize by constructing lam full every iter. Okay.

`mirror_update_from_grad(a, grad, beta)`:
```
def mirror_update_from_grad(a, grad, beta):
    maxg = grad.max()
    if maxg <= 1e-300: return a
    d = grad / maxg
    # Clip d? nonnegative. If some very large? normalized max1.
    fac = np.exp(-beta * d)
    an = a * fac
    s = an.sum()
    if s <=1e-300: return a
    return an / s
```
If a has zeros, stays.

def smooth_random_init(n, rng, seed=None):
    t=np.linspace(0,1,n,endpoint=False)
    a=np.ones(n)
    # random low frequencies powers of 2 up to 31
    freqs=np.arange(1,33)
    amps=np.exp(-freqs/8.0) * 0.6
    ph=rng.uniform(0,2π,32)
    for f,amp,phi in zip(freqs,amps,ph):
       a += amp*np.sin(2π*f*t + phi)

    a=np.maximum(a,0.01)
    return normalize_sum1(a)

def coordinate_best_q(a, c, S, i, q_values, n):
    old=a[i]
    if old <=0:
        return 1.0
    lo=i
    hi=min(2*n-2, i+n-1)
    length=hi-lo+1
    base=c[lo:hi+1]
    partner=np.arange(length, dtype=np.int64) + (lo - i)
    coeff=a[partner]
    dq = q_values - 1.0
    # qshape
    vals = base[np.newaxis, :].astype(np.float64, copy=True)
    vals += (2.0*old*dq)[:, None] * coeff[np.newaxis, :]
    diag = 2*i - lo
    if 0 <= diag < length:
        xold = old
        vals[:, diag] += (xold*dq)[:, None]? Actually scalars: (old*dq)**2 for each candidate; do vals[:, diag] += (old*dq)**2
    max_aff = vals.max(axis=1)
    # unaffected
    unmax=0.0
    if lo>0: unmax=max(unmax, float(c[:lo].max()))
    if hi+1 < c.size: unmax=max(unmax, float(c[hi+1:].max()))
    ns = S + old*dq
    valid = ns > 1e-300
    scores = np.full_like(dq, np.inf)
    scores[valid] = (2.0*n) * np.maximum(max_aff[valid], unmax) / (ns[valid]*ns[valid])
    # ensure if q=1 gives current score maybe but due c numeric; we can keep.
    idx=int(np.argmin(scores))
    return float(q_values[idx])

def coordinate_select(a,c,top_lags,T,rng):

def coordinate_optimize(a, n, budget, rng, q_values, target_score, initial_score):
    a=normalize_sum1(a)
    c=fft_conv(a); S=1; score=2n*c.max(); best=score; best_a=a.copy()
    rounds=0
    while time<budget:
       # top lags
       lag_idx = np.argpartition(c, -5)[:5]
       lag_vals = c[lag_idx]
       order=np.argsort(lag_vals)[::-1]
       top_lags=lag_idx[order]
       selected = select_top_coords(a,c,top_lags, max(20, 20+rounds//10), rng)
       any_improve=False
       for i in selected:
          old_score = score # from current c/S
          unmax = 0
          if i>0: unmax = c[:i].max()
          if c[i+n:].size: unmax=max(unmax, c[i+n:].max())
          q,best_q_score = coordinate_best_q(...)
          if best_q_score < old_score* (1 - 1e-10):
              apply
              score=best_q_score (or recompute max? unmax and affected for chosen q, but after other changes okay)
              rescaled? We can let S drift; score formula accurate. But a/c scale can drift after many updates.
              any_improve=True
              if score<best: best=score; best_a=a/S? Wait best_a should be normalized? For score invariant, store a current unnormalized. At end normalize best_a.
          # Maybe if S not near1, rescale to avoid overflow:
          if S > 2 or S < 0.5:
              a *= 1/S; c *= 1/(S*S); S=1.0
              score = 2n*c.max() (same)
       if not any_improve:
           # perturb selected/random
           if len(selected):
               i = selected[rng.integers(len(selected))]
               # try random q from wider list
           else:
              # random coordinate
           # or multiplicative noise and then coordinate?
       rounds +=1
       if best <= target_score*0.99 and ... maybe continue
    return normalize_sum1(best_a), best
```

Now mirror run function. Need implement carefully to avoid infinite loops/time.

Pseudo:
```
def run_mirror_opt(a_init, L, n, time_deadline, rng, target_score, initial_beta=0.05, initial_k=16, do_line_search=False):
    a=normalize_sum1(a_init)
    c=fft_conv(a,L); score=2*n*c.max(); best=score; best_a=a.copy()
    beta=initial_beta
    it=0; stall=0
    ema=np.zeros(n)
    m=0.2
    while time.monotonic() < time_deadline:
       it+=1
       # update best
       cmax=c.max()
       score=2*n*cmax
       if score < best - 1e-12:
           best=score; best_a=a.copy(); stall=0
       else:
           stall+=1
       if best <= target_score*0.995 and stall>1000: # maybe continue but can break? Continue for better.
       if it % 2000 ==0: print
       k=min(512, initial_k + it//20)
       active, weights = compute_top_weights(c, k)
       grad = gradient_from_weights(a,L,active,weights,n)
       maxg=grad.max()
       if not finite or maxg<=1e-20: break
       d=grad/maxg
       ema = (1-m)*ema + m*d
       maxe=ema.max()
       if maxe<=1e-20: break
       d_use=ema/maxe
       # beta adapt
       if stall > 50:
          beta = min(1.0, max(0.01, beta*0.8))
       if stall > 200:
          beta = rng.uniform(0.02,0.5)
          if rng.random()<0.3:
             # reset ema direction to current grad
             ema=d.copy()
       # try 1 or 2 betas
       an=a*exp(-beta*d_use)
       s=an.sum(); if s>0 an/=s
       cn=fft_conv(an,L); sc=2n*cn.max()
       if sc < score or (sc < best + 0.005 and rng.random()<0.1):
           a=an; c=cn
           if sc < score: beta=min(2.0,beta*1.05)
           else: beta=max(0.01,beta*0.9)
       else:
           beta=max(0.01,beta*0.85)
           # occasionally larger step? Not now
    return normalize_sum1(best_a), best
```
Potential issue: EMA d_use may not reduce current top peak if stale; but smoothing. Maybe when stall high reset.

Should we accept non-improvement to current to escape? Could. Add if `sc < score * 1.02` and rng random, accept. But careful. Use small tolerance.

Function `select_coords` top-level:
```
def select_coords(a,c,top_lags, max_sel, rng):
    n=len(a)
    seen=np.zeros(n,dtype=bool)
    selected=[]
    ranks=0
    for t in top_lags:
        t=int(t)
        lo=max(0, t-n+1); hi=min(n-1,t)
        if lo>hi: continue
        idx = np.arange(lo, hi+1)
        partner = t - idx
        contrib = a[idx] * a[partner]
        k = min(max_sel - len(selected), idx.size)
        if k <=0: break
        # add top k
        part = np.argpartition(contrib, -k)[:k]
        part = idx[part]
        # sort part by contrib
        order = np.argsort(contrib[part])[::-1]
        part=part[order]
        for ii in part:
           ii=int(ii)
           if not seen[ii]:
              seen[ii]=True; selected.append(ii); ranks+=1
              if ranks>=max_sel: break
        if ranks>=max_sel: break
    if not selected:
       # top contributors to lag with max maybe none? choose random
       selected = rng.choice(n, size=min(max_sel,n), replace=False).tolist()
    return np.array(selected, dtype=np.int64)
```
For ordered contributions, if i=partner (t even), product counted half? Ranking maybe. fine.

`positive_normalize(a, S=1.0)`:
```
def positive_normalize(a, S=1.0):
    a=np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
    s=float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        a = np.ones_like(a)
        s = float(a.size)
    return a * (S/s)
```

`fft_conv(a,L=None)`:
```
def fft_conv(a, L=None):
    n = a.size
    if L is None: L = 1 << (2*n - 2).bit_length()
    A = fft(a, L)
    c = np.real(ifft(A*A))[:2*n-1]
    np.maximum(c,0.0, out=c)
    return c
```
Use `np.maximum` out to modify.
`fft_conv_and_A` similar.

`score_from(c,n,S)`:
```
mx=float(c.max()) if c.size else 0.0
return 2.0*n*mx/(S*S)
```

`top_active_weights(c,k)`:
```
def top_active_weights(c,k):
    m=c.size
    if k<=0 or m==0: return np.empty(0,np.int64), np.empty(0)
    if k>=m:
       idx=np.arange(m,np.int64)
    else:
       idx=np.argpartition(c, m-k, axis=0)[:k]
    vals=c[idx]
    w=vals*vals
    ws=float(w.sum())
    if ws<=0 or not np.isfinite(ws):
       w=np.ones_like(vals,dtype=np.float64)
       ws=w.sum()
    w=w/ws
    return idx,w
```
Argpartition sorts by value; if c contains NaN? no.

`gradient_with_lambda(a, A, L, active, weights, n)`:
```
def gradient_with_lambda(a, A, L, active, weights, n):
    lam=np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
    if active.size:
       lam[active] = weights
    H=fft(lam, L)
    grad=np.real(ifft(H * np.conj(A)))[:n]
    return 2.0*grad
```
We pass A from conv to save. Note `a` not used except n? We can omit.

`smooth_random_init(n,rng, scale=1.0)`: as above.
Maybe ensure sum>0.

`select_coordinates(a,c,top_lags,max_sel,rng)`: as above.

`coordinate_best_q(a,c,S,i,q_values,unmax,n)`:
```
def coordinate_best_q(a,c,S,i,q_values,unmax,n):
    old=float(a[i])
    if old <= 0.0:
        # no improvement possible with zero old; try maybe set to small? skip
        return 1.0, np.inf
    dq = q_values - 1.0
    base = c[i:i+n]
    vals = base[np.newaxis, :].astype(np.float64, copy=True)
    vals += (2.0 * old * dq)[:, np.newaxis] * a[np.newaxis, :]
    vals[:, i] += (old * dq) ** 2
    max_aff = vals.max(axis=1)
    ns = S + old * dq
    scores = np.full(dq.shape, np.inf, dtype=np.float64)
    valid = ns > 1e-300
    if np.any(valid):
        combined = np.maximum(max_aff[valid], unmax)
        scores[valid] = (2.0 * float(n) * combined) / (ns[valid] * ns[valid])
    idx = int(np.argmin(scores))
    return float(q_values[idx]), float(scores[idx])
```
Potential `base[np.newaxis,:]` view, `vals +=` modifies new array; base not. Need dtype if base read-only? okay.
If q_values includes 0 and old very close S, ns could <=0. ok.
Unmax might be negative? c clipped nonnegative.

`coordinate_apply(a,c,S,i,q,n)`:
```
def coordinate_apply(a,c,S,i,q,n):
    old=float(a[i]); x=old*q; d=x-old
    if abs(d) <= 0.0 or d == 0.0: return S
    # clip x nonnegative
    if x < 0: x=0; d=x-old
    c[i:i+n] += 2.0*d*a  # uses old a at i
    if 2*i < c.size:
       c[2*i] += d*d
    a[i]=x
    S += d
    return S
```

`optimize_coordinate(a_init, L, n, time_deadline, rng, target_score, q_values)`:

```
def optimize_coordinate(a_init, L, n, time_deadline, rng, target_score=1.5, q_values=None):
   if q_values is None: q_values=DEFAULT_Q
   a=positive_normalize(a_init,1.0)
   S=1.0
   c=fft_conv(a,L)
   score=2*n*c.max()
   best=score; best_a=a.copy()
   it=0; rounds_no_improve=0
   while time.monotonic() < time_deadline:
      # recalc? c consistent
      score=2*n*c.max()/(S*S) # S maybe not1
      if score < best:
          best_a=a/S; best=score
      # print every 200
      if best <= target_score*0.999 and it > 2000 and (it % 1000 ==0):
          # continue anyway
      # top lags
      m=2*n-1
      klags = 5
      if m > klags:
         idx = np.argpartition(c, m-klags)[:klags]
      else: idx=np.arange(m)
      vals=c[idx]; order=np.argsort(vals)[::-1]; top_lags=idx[order]
      n_sel = 20 + min(30, it//20)
      selected = select_coordinates(a,c,top_lags,n_sel,rng)
      any_improve=False
      for ii in selected:
         i=int(ii)
         if a[i] <= 0: continue
         unmax=0.0
         if i>0:
            unmax = float(c[:i].max())
         rem = i+n
         if rem < c.size:
            mr = float(c[rem:].max())
            if mr > unmax: unmax=mr
         q, qscore = coordinate_best_q(a,c,S,i,q_values,unmax,n)
         if np.isfinite(qscore) and qscore < score*(1.0 - 1e-12):
             S = coordinate_apply(a,c,S,i,q,n)
             score = qscore
             any_improve=True
             if abs(S-1.0) > 0.5 or S<0.5 or S>2.0:
                 a *= 1.0/S; c *= 1.0/(S*S); S=1.0; score=2*n*c.max()
             if score < best:
                 best=score; best_a=a.copy() # S1 if rescaled? If not rescaled, best_a sum maybe not1. Need normalize: best_a=a/S
         # time check inside maybe
         if it%... no
      if not any_improve:
          rounds_no_improve +=1
          # try random q on a few coords
          rq = np.array([0.0,0.2,0.5,1.0,2.0,5.0]) # creates each time; could global.
          for _ in range(5):
             i=int(rng.integers(n))
             if a[i] <=0: continue
             unmax...; q,qscore=coordinate_best_q(...,rq,unmax,n)
             if qscore < score*(1-1e-12): apply etc.
          if not any_improve:
             # small multiplicative noise + recompute
             noise = np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.05, n))
             a = a * noise
             a = np.maximum(a,0.0)
             s = float(a.sum())
             if s >0:
                 a = a/(s)
                 S=1.0
                 c=fft_conv(a,L)
                 score=2*n*c.max()
                 if score < best: update
      else:
          rounds_no_improve =0
      if it % 50 ==0:
          # recompute to fix drift
          s=float(a.sum())
          if s>0:
             # keep scale s? To avoid changing c scale, recompute with current a: c_new=fft_conv(a,L); S_new=s
             c=fft_conv(a,L); S=s; score=2*n*c.max()/(S*S)
             if score < best: update
      it+=1
   # normalize best_a
   best_a=positive_normalize(best_a,1.0)
   return best_a,best

```
def optimize_mirror(...):
   a=positive_normalize(a_init,1.0); A=fft(a,L); c=...; score=...
   best=score; best_a=a.copy()
   beta=0.05; k=32; it=0; stall=0; ema=np.zeros(n)
   while time<deadline:
      score=2*n*c.max()
      if score<best: ...
      if it%500==0: print
      # active
      k = int(min(512, max(k, 32 + it//30)))? We'll set schedule.
      active, weights = top_active_weights(c,k)
      grad = gradient... using A
      maxg=grad.max()
      if not finite or <=0: break
      d=grad/maxg
      ema = 0.6*ema + 0.4*d
      maxe=ema.max()
      if maxe<=0: ema=d; maxe=maxg
      d_use=ema/maxe
      # betas
      betas = [beta]
      if stall > 50 and beta < 0.8: betas.append(min(1.0, beta*2.0))
      best_sc=score+1; best_an=a
      for bt in betas:
         fac=np.exp(-bt*d_use)
         an=a*fac
         an=np.maximum(an,0.0)
         s=an.sum()
         if s<=0: continue
         an=an/s
         Anew=fft(an,L); cn=real(ifft(Anew*Anew))[:2n-1]; max(cn,0)
         sc=2n*cn.max()
         if sc<best_sc:
            best_sc=sc; best_an=an; best_cn=cn; best_A=Anew
      if best_sc < score * (1.0 - 1e-12) or (best_sc < score*1.005 and rng.random()<0.1):
          a=best_an; c=best_cn; A=best_A
          if best_sc < score: beta=min(1.0, beta*1.05); stall=0
          else: beta=max(0.005,beta*0.9)
      else:
          beta=max(0.005,beta*0.85); stall+=1
      if stall > 100:
          # perturb
          sigma = 0.01 + 0.1*min(1.0, stall/1000.0)
          a = a * np.exp(rng.normal(0, sigma, n))
          a = positive_normalize(a,1.0)
          A=fft(a,L); c=fft...
          sc=... update best?
          stall=0; beta=0.05; k=int(rng.choice([16,64,256,512]))
      it+=1
   return positive_normalize(best_a,1.0), best

Now, random smooth init. Let's implement:
```
def smooth_random_init(n, rng, order=8):
    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, endpoint=False)
    a = 1.0 + 0.5*(t - 0.5) * rng.uniform(-1,1)
    nterms = 32
    f = np.arange(1, nterms+1)
    amp = 0.3 / np.sqrt(f)
    ph = rng.uniform(0.0, 2*np.pi, nterms)
    for fi, ai, pi in zip(f, amp, ph):
        a += ai * np.sin(2*np.pi*fi*t + pi)
    a = np.maximum(a, 1e-3)
    return positive_normalize(a,1.0)
```

def optimize_mirror(...):
    a = positive_normalize(a_init,1.0)
    A = fft(a,L)
    c = np.real(ifft(A*A))[:2*n-1]
    np.maximum(c,0.0,out=c)
    score = 2*n*c.max()
    best=score; best_a=a.copy()
    beta=0.05; k=32; it=0; stall=0
    ema=np.zeros(n)
    while time.monotonic() < time_deadline:
       # update best score? c consistent
       if score < best: best=score; best_a=a.copy()
       if it % 1000 ==0: print
       active, weights = top_active_weights(c, k)
       if active.size ==0: break
       grad = gradient_with_lambda(A, L, active, weights, n)
       maxg = grad.max()
       if not np.isfinite(maxg) or maxg <= 1e-30: break
       d = grad / maxg
       ema = 0.6*ema + 0.4*d
       maxe = ema.max()
       if maxe <= 1e-30:
          ema = d.copy(); maxe = ema.max()
       d_use = ema / maxe
       # possible no finite
       betas = [beta]
       if stall > 50 and beta < 0.8:
           betas.append(min(1.0, beta*2.0))
       best_sc = score + 1.0
       best_an = None; best_cn=None; best_A=None
       for bt in betas:
           an = a * np.exp(-bt * d_use)
           an = np.maximum(an, 0.0)
           s = float(an.sum())
           if s <= 1e-30: continue
           an = an / s
           Anew = fft(an, L)
           cn = np.real(ifft(Anew * Anew))[:2*n-1]
           np.maximum(cn,0.0,out=cn)
           sc = 2.0*n*float(cn.max())
           if sc < best_sc:
               best_sc=sc; best_an=an; best_cn=cn; best_A=Anew
       if best_an is not None and (best_sc < score or rng.random()<0.05):
           a=best_an; c=best_cn; A=best_A
           if best_sc < score:
              stall=0; beta=min(1.0,beta*1.05)
           else:
              stall+=1; beta=max(0.005,beta*0.95)
       else:
           stall +=1
           beta=max(0.005,beta*0.85)
       if stall > 150:
           sigma = min(0.2, 0.02 + 0.02*(stall//50))
           a = a * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sigma, n))
           a = a + (1.0/n)*1e-3*rng.random(n)
           a = positive_normalize(a,1.0)
           A=fft(a,L); c=...; score=...
           if score < best: best=score; best_a=a.copy()
           stall=0; beta=0.05
           k = int([16,32,64,128,256,512][rng.integers(6)])
           ema=np.zeros(n)
       # update k
       k = int(min(512, 32 + it//30))
       it += 1
    best_a = positive_normalize(best_a,1.0)
    # final score of best_a? maybe use best
    return best_a,best

```python
"""
Search for non-negative step heights minimizing
    2*n*max(convolve(a,a)) / (sum(a))**2.

The optimizer replaces the previous spectral autocorrelation heuristic with:
  1) exact FFT evaluation of the linear convolution a*a,
  2) mirror-descent / multiplicative updates driven by exact subgradients of
     top active convolution peaks,
  3) fast vectorized coordinate descent on the homogeneous objective,
  4) multiple smooth random starts and polishing of the provided SOTA sequence.

The returned sequence is scaled to sum 10, which is safe for the evaluator's
sum and max-entry checks while leaving the score unchanged.
"""

import time
import numpy as np
from scipy.fft import fft, ifft

DEFAULT_Q = np.array([
    0.0, 0.005, 0.02, 0.05, 0.10, 0.20,
    0.35, 0.50, 0.70, 0.90, 1.00,
    1.15, 1.40, 1.80, 2.50
], dtype=np.float64)

FINE_Q = np.array([
    0.80, 0.90, 0.95, 0.97, 0.99,
    1.00, 1.01, 1.03, 1.07, 1.15, 1.30
], dtype=np.float64)

RANDOM_Q = np.array([0.0, 0.1, 0.3, 0.6, 1.0, 1.8, 3.5], dtype=np.float64)


def positive_normalize(a, target_sum=1.0):
    """Clip to non-negative and rescale to a target sum."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    a[np.isnan(a)] = 0.0
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        a = np.ones_like(a, dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(a.size)
    return a * (float(target_sum) / s)


def fft_linear_convolution(a, L=None):
    """
    Exact linear convolution a*a via FFT.
    a*a is the product of DFTs, not |DFT(a)|^2.
    """
    n = int(a.size)
    if L is None:
        L = 1 << (2 * n - 2).bit_length()
    A = fft(a, L)
    c = np.real(ifft(A * A))
    c = c[:2 * n - 1]
    np.maximum(c, 0.0, out=c)
    return c


def fft_linear_convolution_and_fft(a, L=None):
    """Return (c, A) where c = linear convolution a*a and A = fft(a)."""
    n = int(a.size)
    if L is None:
        L = 1 << (2 * n - 2).bit_length()
    A = fft(a, L)
    c = np.real(ifft(A * A))
    c = c[:2 * n - 1]
    np.maximum(c, 0.0, out=c)
    return c, A


def score_from_convolution(c, n, S=1.0):
    """Homogeneous score 2*n*max(c)/S^2."""
    if c.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    mx = float(c.max())
    if not np.isfinite(mx) or mx < 0.0:
        mx = 0.0
    S = float(S)
    if not np.isfinite(S) or S <= 1e-300:
        return np.inf
    return 2.0 * float(n) * mx / (S * S)


def top_active_weights(c, k):
    """
    Choose top-k convolution lags and non-negative weights normalized to sum 1.
    Weighting is proportional to the peak values, which focuses the subgradient
    on the highest lags while still considering a plateau.
    """
    m = int(c.size)
    if m == 0 or k <= 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.int64), np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if k >= m:
        idx = np.arange(m, dtype=np.int64)
    else:
        idx = np.argpartition(c, m - k)[:k]
    idx = idx.astype(np.int64, copy=False)
    vals = c[idx].astype(np.float64, copy=False)
    w = vals
    ws = float(w.sum())
    if ws <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(ws):
        w = vals * vals
        ws = float(w.sum())
    if ws <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(ws):
        w = np.ones_like(vals, dtype=np.float64)
        ws = float(w.sum())
    if ws <= 1e-300:
        return idx, w
    return idx, w / ws


def gradient_from_top_weights(A, L, active, weights, n):
    """
    Exact subgradient of sum_t lambda_t * (a*a)_t.
    If r_i = sum_j a_j lambda_{i+j}, then gradient = 2*r.
    """
    lam = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
    if active.size:
        lam[active] = weights
    H = fft(lam, L)
    r = np.real(ifft(H * np.conj(A)))[:n]
    return 2.0 * r


def smooth_random_init(n, rng, strength=0.75):
    """Smooth non-negative random initialization with asymmetric low-frequency content."""
    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, endpoint=False)
    a = 1.0 + 0.25 * (t - 0.5) * rng.uniform(-1.0, 1.0)
    f = np.arange(1, 40, dtype=np.int64)
    amp = strength * (0.35 / np.sqrt(f.astype(np.float64)))
    ph = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi, size=f.size)
    for fi, ai, pi in zip(f, amp, ph):
        a += ai * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * float(fi) * t + pi)
    # Add a bit of mid-scale roughness to avoid overly smooth local minima.
    a += 0.05 * rng.standard_normal(n)
    return positive_normalize(a, 1.0)


def select_coordinates(a, c, top_lags, max_sel, rng):
    """
    Select coordinates with large contribution to one of the current top lags.
    """
    n = int(a.size)
    seen = np.zeros(n, dtype=bool)
    selected = []

    for lag in top_lags:
        if len(selected) >= max_sel:
            break
        t = int(lag)
        lo = max(0, t - n + 1)
        hi = min(n - 1, t)
        if lo > hi:
            continue
        idx = np.arange(lo, hi + 1, dtype=np.int64)
        partner = t - idx
        contrib = a[idx] * a[partner]
        k = min(max_sel - len(selected), int(idx.size))
        if k <= 0:
            continue
        part = np.argpartition(contrib, -k)[:k]
        part = idx[part]
        order = np.argsort(contrib[part])[::-1]
        part = part[order]
        for ii in part:
            ii = int(ii)
            if not seen[ii]:
                seen[ii] = True
                selected.append(ii)
                if len(selected) >= max_sel:
                    break

    if not selected:
        selected = rng.choice(n, size=min(max_sel, n), replace=False).astype(np.int64).tolist()
    return np.asarray(selected, dtype=np.int64)


def coordinate_best_multiplier(a, c, S, i, q_values, unmax, n):
    """
    For coordinate i, evaluate a small grid of multipliers q exactly using
    the current convolution c.  Returns (q, score).
    """
    old = float(a[i])
    if old <= 1e-300:
        return 1.0, np.inf

    old = float(old)
    dq = q_values - 1.0
    base = c[i:i + n]

    # affected[k] = c[i+k] + 2*d*a[k] + d^2 if k == i
    vals = base[np.newaxis, :].astype(np.float64, copy=True)
    vals += (2.0 * old * dq)[:, np.newaxis] * a[np.newaxis, :]
    vals[:, i] += (old * dq) ** 2

    max_aff = vals.max(axis=1)
    ns = S + old * dq
    scores = np.full(dq.shape, np.inf, dtype=np.float64)
    valid = ns > 1e-300
    if np.any(valid):
        combined = np.maximum(max_aff[valid], unmax)
        scores[valid] = (2.0 * float(n) * combined) / (ns[valid] * ns[valid])

    if not np.any(np.isfinite(scores)):
        return 1.0, np.inf
    best_idx = int(np.argmin(scores))
    return float(q_values[best_idx]), float(scores[best_idx])


def coordinate_apply_update(a, c, S, i, q, n):
    """Apply an exact coordinate update.  Returns updated S."""
    old = float(a[i])
    new = old * float(q)
    d = new - old
    if abs(d) <= 1e-300:
        return S

    if new < 0.0:
        new = 0.0
        d = new - old

    # a[i] is still old here, which is required for the linear update terms.
    c[i:i + n] += 2.0 * d * a
    if 2 * i < c.size:
        c[2 * i] += d * d

    a[i] = new
    return S + d


def optimize_coordinate_descent(a_init, L, n, time_deadline, rng,
                                target_score=1.50, q_values=None):
    """
    Fast homogeneous coordinate descent.  It optimizes max(c) / sum(a)^2
    directly, so the scale of a is allowed to drift and is periodically normalized.
    """
    if q_values is None:
        q_values = DEFAULT_Q

    a = positive_normalize(a_init, 1.0)
    S = 1.0
    c = fft_linear_convolution(a, L)
    score = score_from_convolution(c, n, S)
    best = score
    best_a = a.copy()

    it = 0
    stall = 0

    while time.monotonic() < time_deadline:
        np.maximum(c, 0.0, out=c)
        score = score_from_convolution(c, n, S)
        if score < best:
            best = score
            best_a = a / S if S > 0 else positive_normalize(a, 1.0)

        if it % 1000 == 0:
            print("    coord iter %6d score %.8f best %.8f" % (it, score, best), flush=True)

        m = int(c.size)
        klags = 5 if m > 5 else m
        if klags >= m:
            idx = np.arange(m, dtype=np.int64)
        else:
            idx = np.argpartition(c, m - klags)[:klags]
        vals = c[idx]
        order = np.argsort(vals)[::-1]
        top_lags = idx[order]

        n_sel = 24 + min(28, it // 40)
        selected = select_coordinates(a, c, top_lags, n_sel, rng)

        # Use a finer multiplier grid when already near the target.
        if score < target_score + 0.045:
            q_use = FINE_Q
        else:
            q_use = q_values

        improved = False

        for ii in selected:
            if time.monotonic() > time_deadline:
                break
            i = int(ii)
            oldval = float(a[i])
            if oldval <= 1e-300:
                continue

            unmax = 0.0
            if i > 0:
                unmax = float(c[:i].max())
            rem = i + n
            if rem < c.size:
                mr = float(c[rem:].max())
                if mr > unmax:
                    unmax = mr

            q, qscore = coordinate_best_multiplier(a, c, S, i, q_use, unmax, n)
            if np.isfinite(qscore) and qscore < score * (1.0 - 1e-12):
                S = coordinate_apply_update(a, c, S, i, q, n)
                score = qscore
                improved = True

                # Keep the scale in a numerically convenient range.
                if S < 0.5 or S > 2.0:
                    if S > 1e-300:
                        a *= 1.0 / S
                        c *= 1.0 / (S * S)
                        S = 1.0
                    score = score_from_convolution(c, n, S)
                    if score < best:
                        best = score
                        best_a = a.copy()

        if not improved:
            # Try a few random coordinates with a wider multiplier grid.
            for _ in range(6):
                if time.monotonic() > time_deadline:
                    break
                i = int(rng.integers(n))
                if float(a[i]) <= 1e-300:
                    continue
                unmax = 0.0
                if i > 0:
                    unmax = float(c[:i].max())
                rem = i + n
                if rem < c.size:
                    mr = float(c[rem:].max())
                    if mr > unmax:
                        unmax = mr
                q, qscore = coordinate_best_multiplier(a, c, S, i, RANDOM_Q, unmax, n)
                if np.isfinite(qscore) and qscore < score * (1.0 - 1e-12):
                    S = coordinate_apply_update(a, c, S, i, q, n)
                    score = qscore
                    improved = True
                    if S < 0.5 or S > 2.0:
                        if S > 1e-300:
                            a *= 1.0 / S
                            c *= 1.0 / (S * S)
                            S = 1.0
                        score = score_from_convolution(c, n, S)
                        if score < best:
                            best = score
                            best_a = a.copy()

            if not improved:
                stall += 1
                sigma = 0.015
                if stall > 40:
                    sigma = 0.04
                if stall > 120:
                    sigma = 0.08
                if stall > 300:
                    sigma = 0.15

                a = a * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sigma, n))
                # Add a tiny positive floor so zero coordinates can be re-excited.
                a += (1.0 / float(n)) * 2e-4 * rng.random(n)
                a = positive_normalize(a, 1.0)
                S = 1.0
                c = fft_linear_convolution(a, L)
                score = score_from_convolution(c, n, S)
                if score < best:
                    best = score
                    best_a = a.copy()
                stall = 0
        else:
            stall = 0

        # Periodically correct accumulated floating-point drift in c.
        if it % 100 == 99:
            Sout = float(a.sum())
            if Sout > 1e-300:
                c = fft_linear_convolution(a, L)
                score = score_from_convolution(c, n, Sout)
                if score < best:
                    best = score
                    best_a = a / Sout

        it += 1

    best_a = positive_normalize(best_a, 1.0)
    return best_a, best


def optimize_mirror_descent(a_init, L, n, time_deadline, rng,
                            target_score=1.50):
    """
    Multiplicative mirror descent in the non-negative simplex.
    Uses exact subgradients of the top active convolution lags.
    """
    a = positive_normalize(a_init, 1.0)
    c, A = fft_linear_convolution_and_fft(a, L)
    score = score_from_convolution(c, n, 1.0)
    best = score
    best_a = a.copy()

    beta = 0.06
    k = 32
    it = 0
    stall = 0
    ema = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    while time.monotonic() < time_deadline:
        if score < best:
            best = score
            best_a = a.copy()

        if it % 500 == 0:
            print("    mirror iter %6d score %.8f best %.8f beta %.4f" % (
                it, score, best, beta), flush=True)

        active, weights = top_active_weights(c, k)
        if active.size == 0:
            break

        grad = gradient_from_top_weights(A, L, active, weights, n)
        maxg = float(grad.max())
        if not np.isfinite(maxg) or maxg <= 1e-30:
            break

        d = grad / maxg
        ema = 0.6 * ema + 0.4 * d
        maxe = float(ema.max())
        if not np.isfinite(maxe) or maxe <= 1e-30:
            ema = d.copy()
            maxe = float(ema.max())
        d_use = ema / maxe

        betas = [beta]
        if stall > 60 and beta < 0.9:
            betas.append(min(1.2, beta * 2.0))

        best_sc = score + 1.0
        best_an = None
        best_cn = None
        best_A = None

        for bt in betas:
            if time.monotonic() > time_deadline:
                break
            an = a * np.exp(-bt * d_use)
            an = np.maximum(an, 0.0)
            s = float(an.sum())
            if s <= 1e-30:
                continue
            an = an / s
            cn, Anew = fft_linear_convolution_and_fft(an, L)
            sc = score_from_convolution(cn, n, 1.0)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc
                best_an = an
                best_cn = cn
                best_A = Anew

        accepted = False
        if best_an is not None and (
                best_sc < score or (best_sc < score * 1.01 and rng.random() < 0.25)):
            a = best_an
            c = best_cn
            A = best_A
            score = best_sc
            accepted = True
            if score < best:
                best = score
                best_a = a.copy()
            if best_sc < score - 1e-15:
                stall = 0
                beta = min(1.2, beta * 1.04)
            else:
                stall += 1
                beta = max(0.006, beta * 0.94)
        else:
            stall += 1
            beta = max(0.006, beta * 0.85)

        # Escape move: multiplicative noise plus tiny uniform floor.
        if stall > 120:
            sigma = min(0.22, 0.025 + 0.025 * int(stall // 60))
            a = a * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sigma, n))
            a += (1.0 / float(n)) * 2e-4 * rng.random(n)
            a = positive_normalize(a, 1.0)
            c, A = fft_linear_convolution_and_fft(a, L)
            score = score_from_convolution(c, n, 1.0)
            if score < best:
                best = score
                best_a = a.copy()
            stall = 0
            beta = 0.06
            ema = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
            k = int([16, 32, 64, 128, 256, 512][int(rng.integers(6))])

        # Increase plateau awareness as the run proceeds.
        k = int(min(512, 32 + it // 25))
        it += 1

    best_a = positive_normalize(best_a, 1.0)
    return best_a, best


def prepare_candidate_length_and_init():
    """
    Choose a length and a strong initialization from the previous construction,
    if available, otherwise default to 8192.
    """
    n = 8192
    prev = None
    try:
        h = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
        h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1000.0)
        h[np.isnan(h)] = 0.0
        if h.size >= 256 and float(h.sum()) > 0.0:
            if 256 <= h.size <= 16384:
                n = int(h.size)
                prev = h.copy()
            elif h.size > 16384:
                # Downsample by averaging consecutive blocks if possible.
                target = 8192
                if h.size % target == 0:
                    block = h.size // target
                    prev = np.mean(h.reshape(target, block), axis=1)
                    n = target
                else:
                    # Use the central 8192 entries as a fallback.
                    seg = h[h.size // 2 - target // 2: h.size // 2 - target // 2 + target]
                    if seg.size == target and float(seg.sum()) > 0.0:
                        prev = seg.copy()
                        n = target
    except Exception:
        prev = None

    if prev is None or float(np.sum(np.clip(prev, 0.0, 1000.0))) <= 0.0:
        n = 8192
        prev = None

    return n, prev


def next_power_of_two_conv_len(n):
    """FFT length for linear convolution of length n with itself."""
    return 1 << (2 * n - 2).bit_length()


def fallback_sequence():
    n = 256
    return [10.0 / float(n) for _ in range(n)]


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400):
    """
    Search for a sequence minimizing the evaluator.  Returns a Python list.
    """
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    budget_s = max(1.0, float(budget_s))
    deadline = t0 + max(0.0, budget_s - 30.0)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))

    try:
        n, prev = prepare_candidate_length_and_init()
        L = next_power_of_two_conv_len(n)
        print("Optimizing length n=%d, FFT L=%d, budget=%.0fs" % (n, L, budget_s), flush=True)

        inits = []

        if prev is not None:
            a0 = positive_normalize(prev, 1.0)
            c0 = fft_linear_convolution(a0, L)
            s0 = score_from_convolution(c0, n, 1.0)
            inits.append(("prev", a0, s0))
            print("  prev init score=%.8f" % s0, flush=True)

        # Uniform is a safe baseline (score 2).
        a1 = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
        inits.append(("uniform", a1, 2.0))

        # A few random smooth starts for global exploration.
        for r in range(4):
            ar = smooth_random_init(n, rng, strength=0.55 + 0.12 * r)
            cr = fft_linear_convolution(ar, L)
            sr = score_from_convolution(cr, n, 1.0)
            inits.append(("rand%d" % r, ar, sr))

        # Sort initializations by current internal score, but keep prev first if it
        # is already very good.
        inits.sort(key=lambda x: x[2])

        global_a = None
        global_score = np.inf

        for name, a, sc in inits:
            if np.isfinite(sc) and (global_score is None or sc < global_score):
                global_score = sc
                global_a = positive_normalize(a, 1.0)

        if global_a is None:
            global_a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
            global_score = 2.0

        print("Best initial internal score=%.8f" % global_score, flush=True)

        # Phase 1: mirror descent from the best few starts.
        phase1_total = (deadline - time.monotonic()) * 0.16
        phase1_deadline = min(deadline, time.monotonic() + max(30.0, phase1_total))
        portions = [0.45, 0.25, 0.20, 0.10]

        for rank, (name, a, sc) in enumerate(inits[:4]):
            rem = phase1_deadline - time.monotonic()
            if rem < 15.0:
                break
            portion = portions[rank] if rank < len(portions) else 0.10
            budget = min(rem, max(10.0, phase1_total * portion))
            if budget <= 5.0:
                continue
            print("  mirror phase %s budget=%.0fs" % (name, budget), flush=True)
            an, sn = optimize_mirror_descent(a, L, n, time.monotonic() + budget, rng)
            if np.isfinite(sn) and sn < global_score:
                global_score = sn
                global_a = positive_normalize(an, 1.0)
                print("    new global score=%.8f" % global_score, flush=True)

        # Phase 2: main coordinate-descent polish on the best sequence.
        now = time.monotonic()
        phase2_total = (deadline - now) * 0.52
        phase2_deadline = min(deadline, now + max(60.0, phase2_total))
        print("  coordinate phase main budget=%.0fs" % (phase2_deadline - now), flush=True)
        an, sn = optimize_coordinate_descent(global_a, L, n, phase2_deadline, rng)
        if np.isfinite(sn) and sn < global_score:
            global_score = sn
            global_a = positive_normalize(an, 1.0)
            print("  main coordinate best=%.8f" % global_score, flush=True)

        # Phase 3: mirror refinement after coordinate polish.
        now = time.monotonic()
        phase3_total = (deadline - now) * 0.20
        phase3_deadline = min(deadline, now + max(20.0, phase3_total))
        if phase3_deadline > now + 5.0:
            print("  mirror phase final budget=%.0fs" % (phase3_deadline - now), flush=True)
            an, sn = optimize_mirror_descent(global_a, L, n, phase3_deadline, rng)
            if np.isfinite(sn) and sn < global_score:
                global_score = sn
                global_a = positive_normalize(an, 1.0)
                print("    final mirror best=%.8f" % global_score, flush=True)

        # Phase 4: final fine coordinate polish.
        now = time.monotonic()
        phase4_deadline = min(deadline, now + max(10.0, (deadline - now) * 0.65))
        if phase4_deadline > now + 5.0:
            print("  coordinate phase final budget=%.0fs" % (phase4_deadline - now), flush=True)
            an, sn = optimize_coordinate_descent(global_a, L, n, phase4_deadline, rng)
            if np.isfinite(sn) and sn < global_score:
                global_score = sn
                global_a = positive_normalize(an, 1.0)
                print("    final coordinate best=%.8f" % global_score, flush=True)

        # Final safe scaling.  Sum 10 is safely above the evaluator's 0.01 sum floor,
        # and max <= 10 is far below the 1000 clamp.
        out = positive_normalize(global_a, 10.0)
        out = np.maximum(out, 0.0)
        out = np.clip(out, 0.0, 1000.0)
        answer = [float(x) for x in out]

        # One direct verification.  If somehow invalid, fall back to a known-valid
        # uniform sequence.
        try:
            ver = evaluate_sequence(answer)
            print("Internal best=%.8f, direct verification=%.8f" % (
                global_score, ver if np.isfinite(ver) else float("inf")), flush=True)
            if not np.isfinite(ver):
                ans = fallback_sequence()
                print("Fallback sequence returned because evaluator rejected output.", flush=True)
                return ans
        except Exception as e:
            print("Verification exception: %r" % (e,), flush=True)

        print("Returning length %d, final sum=%.6f, final max=%.6f" % (
            len(answer), float(np.sum(np.asarray(answer, dtype=np.float64))),
            float(np.max(np.asarray(answer, dtype=np.float64)))), flush=True)
        return answer

    except Exception as exc:
        print("propose_candidate failed: %r" % (exc,), flush=True)
        return fallback_sequence()
```
